## LEAF_TARGET_PLAN

| Target | Single operation | Acceptance evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Kiểm thử routed AD-03 cho chi tiết người dùng, lỗi tải và action gate | Add focused page regressions for the current #69 detail flow, signed-int64 route validation, API error-state handling, and DD-gated edit/action controls | AD-03 page spec verifies the exact authenticated #69 detail request, current profile projection, malformed-ID/no-request handling, non-success response gates, and disabled actions without calling unapproved #70–#72 mutations |
| Kiểm thử routed AD-13 cho chi tiết bài học và action gate | Add focused routed page regressions for the current #83 lesson detail projection, route ID guard, failure state, and locked edit action | AD-13 page spec proves the authenticated #83 path, exact source-backed fields, malformed-ID/no-request and failure behavior, and disabled AD-14 handoff without issuing #84–#86 mutations |
| Kiểm thử routed ME-02 cho danh sách khóa học và trạng thái tải | Add page-level regressions for the current #46 list request, supported course projection, loading/empty/error/retry states, and DD-gated controls | Focused routed ME-02 tests prove the exact current #46 request and failure recovery without inventing blocked filters, course metrics, or actions |
| Kiểm thử routed ME-03 cho workspace khóa học và trạng thái tải | Add page-level regressions for current #46/#49/#65 calls, validated workspace projection, route-ID guard, and retry behavior | Focused routed tests prove the real routed request sequence and supported response fields while keeping unapproved mutations/metrics gated |
| Kiểm thử routed ME-04 cho tải tài liệu và tạo bài giảng | Add routed regressions for #46 course selection, #34 LECTURE upload, #47 create payload and source-backed failure/success handling | Focused page tests prove the authenticated requests and exact approved fields without enabling unresolved WYSIWYG, lifecycle or unsupported attachment behavior |
| Sửa ME-03 hiển thị thông báo lỗi rõ ràng khi #49/#65 success envelope sai | Use a generic validation message for successful envelopes with the wrong business code or invalid projection, while preserving server messages for actual `success:false` responses | The page regression confirms #49/#65 malformed-success responses never display their `message` as a validation error and that valid API error envelopes preserve the current failure handling |
| Kiểm thử ST-15 chỉ gửi bài sau xác nhận và khóa gửi lại khi kết quả mơ hồ | Add page-level regressions for explicit confirmation, in-flight duplicate activation, accepted #31 navigation, and no blind retry/navigation after network ambiguity or 409 | The ST-15 page spec passes all four scenarios without asserting the unresolved #30 queue/flush policy |
| Kiểm thử ST15SubmitService ánh xạ attempt ID sang API #31 | Add a service-level request mapping assertion for API #31 and `attempt_id` | The focused service spec confirms exactly one #31 request with the route attempt ID |
| Giảm CSS của ST-03 xuống dưới giới hạn build production | Refactor ST-03 component styling without changing DD-specified behavior | ST-03 CSS stays below the 8 kB error budget, focused ST-03 verification passes, and `npm run build` succeeds |
| Lập crosswalk API hiện tại cho nhóm Shared | Map each routed Shared screen to its current caller, endpoint, and available contract/source evidence | Every Shared screen has a caller status and any API/contract gaps are recorded |
| Lập crosswalk API hiện tại cho nhóm Guest | Map each routed Guest screen/tab to its current caller, endpoint, and available contract/source evidence | Every Guest screen has a caller status and any API/contract gaps are recorded |
| Cập nhật source note API #07 và #10 trong endpoint catalog | Record current API #07 category rejection and API #10 ignored rating filter in the Study Web endpoint catalog | Catalog preserves design-contract fields while accurately recording current local source behavior |
| Tạo UTC chính thức cho AD-02 — DanhSachNguoiDung | Audit the existing AD-02 UTC JSON against all current DD sections and screenshots, resolve structural/source issues, regenerate four-sheet Excel and validator reports | UTC validator and Excel validator pass, all 38 elements and all source events have cases or explicit exceptions, blockers stay marked Chờ xác nhận, and workbook is recorded as a plan pending DD approval |
| Cập nhật source evidence AD-01/AD-24 cho API #101/#102 | Reconcile current report caller/route/source facts in both screen DD packages without changing pending metric, permission, or API design decisions | Current-source notes distinguish AD-01's #101 caller, AD-24's lack of caller, #101's local source, and #102's missing source while preserving the DD blockers |

## SCREEN_PAGE_SPEC_COVERAGE_AUDIT

The initial filename-only inventory via `rg --files apps/study-client/src/app/features` found 83 `pages/*-page.ts` sources, 66 sibling `*-page.spec.ts` files, and 17 sources without a sibling spec. After adding ME-12, AD-03, AD-13, ME-02, and ME-03 coverage, the current inventory is 83 page sources, 71 sibling specs, and 12 sources without a sibling spec. `system-readiness-page.ts` is not composed by `app.routes.ts`, leaving 11 route-backed pages without dedicated page specs:

- Mentor: `ME04/pages/me04-page.ts`, `ME05/pages/me05-page.ts`, `ME06/pages/me06-page.ts`, `ME09/pages/me09-page.ts`, `ME10/pages/me10-page.ts`, `ME11/pages/me11-page.ts`, `ME13/pages/me13-page.ts`, `ME15/pages/me15-page.ts`, `ME16/pages/me16-page.ts`, `ME17/pages/me17-page.ts`, `ME18/pages/me18-page.ts`.

## CURRENT_BEHAVIOR

- Source/config: Angular source contains group route files for Shared, Guest, Student, Mentor and Admin; root `app.routes.ts` composes those five. The unused `system.routes.ts` is not composed. The source declarations were read before their exact paths were added to READ_PLAN; this read-set deviation is recorded for audit.
- Runnable tests: Mentor and Admin route specs explicitly cover all 18/25 screens; the focused Student route spec verifies all 26 screen paths, page components, API ID arrays, and shell modes (10 assertions pass). ST-17 has a routed regression for the unresolved field/CTA suppression.
- Full Study Web verification initially failed: `npm test` reported 62 files passed and 4 files failed, with 12 failed tests out of 261. Current-source-aligned root/API/Student assertions and AD-01 request cleanup are now in place; the later full suite passes 66 files and all 261 tests.
- Runtime wiring: 83 screen packages are indexed; current routes cover Shared SH-01–07, Guest GS-01–03/GS-07 plus GS-04–06 tabs owned by GS-03, Student ST-01–26, Mentor ME-01–18, and Admin AD-01–25. Mentor, Admin, and Student route specs verify all 18/25/26 screen mappings respectively. Route metadata is source-level evidence, not browser/runtime route acceptance.
- Guest API evidence: focused Study Server tests for #07–#12 pass 90 tests. Guest routes/services are fixture-backed locally; API, DB, browser session, object storage and deployment remain unverified.
- Focused runtime tests: the ST-03 screen spec passes all 3 tests, root route spec passes 13/13, and the API registry, Student route, and ST-10 specs pass 19/19 together. Latest full Study Web suite passes 104/104 files and 375/375 tests, including five routed ME-02 and five ME-03 page cases, five routed ST-11 page cases and its #24 service mapping test, the ST-12 list/retry/empty/handoff/URL-guard cases, six ST-18 page cases plus three #33/#34/#35 service mapping cases, six ME-12 page cases plus two #59/#60 service mapping tests, four AD-03 and five AD-13 routed cases; ST-13 page/service specs cover #28/#29 mapping, explicit attempt handoff and ambiguous-create lockout; ST-14 page/service specs cover #28/#30 mapping and response states; ST-16 page/service specs pass 8 cases; ST-17/ST-19 policy gates plus ST-20–ST-26 mapping/page tests pass.
- Study Server tests: the full suite passes 1,563 tests after aligning API #26 fixtures and adding route-level API #37/#39 coverage. One existing Starlette/httpx TestClient deprecation warning remains; tests use mocked DB reads and do not verify deployment.
- Production build: latest `npm run build` exits 0 with a 474.39 kB initial bundle (95.98 kB estimated transfer). Eighteen component styles exceed the 4 kB warning budget, including ST-25 at 5.16 kB; none exceeds the 8 kB error budget. ST-26 is below its warning threshold.
- Latest production build succeeds. Initial bundle is 474.39 kB (95.98 kB estimated transfer), below the 500 kB warning budget; 18 component styles report the 4 kB warning, while none exceeds the 8 kB error budget. The Student lazy route chunk is 697.84 kB raw (58.24 kB estimated transfer) and Admin is 385.34 kB raw (65.99 kB estimated transfer). ST-03 remains below the 8 kB error budget.

## SHARED_API_CROSSWALK

| Screen | Current route and caller | Endpoint behavior verified from source | DD/test evidence and unresolved release gap |
|---|---|---|---|
| SH-01 | `/`; `SH01CatalogService` uses `StudyApiService` for #05 `GET /api/v1/categories` and #06 `GET /api/v1/courses?page=1&size=6`. Requests are public and independently present loading/error/retry state. | Server routes #05/#06 are registered; #05 returns category pagination, #06 returns public course pagination. The client validates envelopes/page data before rendering. | Current SH-01 DD allows this public scope; server focused tests cover #05/#06. A previous local dev proxy attempt returned 502; live API, database, and deployment remain unverified. |
| SH-02 | `/login`; `SH02LoginService` calls public #03 `POST /auth/login`. Student login stores its in-memory session and calls #04 `GET /users/me` to confirm profile email/role before entering `/student`; Mentor/Admin sessions route to their homes. | #03 currently returns HTTP 200 `DESIGN_RESOURCE_RETRIEVED` `LoginData` with access/refresh tokens after requiring ACTIVE status and persisting a refresh-token hash. #04 is Student-only and returns a safe profile. | DD expects role-safe login. Client/server focused tests cover envelopes and role dispatch; browser and deployed auth/session persistence remain unverified. `list_endpoint.md` previously described #03 as 201 `UserProfile`; corrected from current source/canonical API catalog. |
| SH-03 | `/register`; `SH03RegistrationService` calls public #01 `POST /auth/register` with `{email,password,full_name}`. It keeps the returned id/email in a one-use in-memory handoff only after the explicit continue action. | #01 returns HTTP 201 `DESIGN_RESOURCE_CREATED` `UserProfile`; current handler creates the user but defers verification-email dispatch. | DD says not to claim email delivery/verification. Client tests and root route integration verify the request and handoff; no ID/email is placed in the URL or persistent browser storage. |
| SH-04 | `/register/verification`; `SH04VerificationService` calls public #02 `POST /auth/verify-email/send` only after an explicit click, with `{user_id,email}` and no Bearer token. | #02 returns HTTP 202 `DESIGN_OPERATION_ACCEPTED` with status `accepted`; its configured provider is a stub and sends no email or OTP/link. No confirmation endpoint is present. | DD release gate remains open. UI explicitly states acceptance does not mean delivery or verification; client feature and root route tests assert this. Actual email verification is not production-ready. |
| SH-05 | `/forbidden`; state comes from Student API #04 authentication/access failure context through `StudentApiService` and `SH05ErrorContextService`; SH-05 has no endpoint of its own. | Student API transport attempts the registered refresh behavior once for a 401, then routes forbidden/auth failures with safe error context; the SH-05 page renders the supplied state without replaying the protected request. | DD defines an auth/error state, not a data endpoint. Current client tests pass; real token expiry/refresh flow against deployed auth is unverified. |
| SH-06 | `/not-found`; shared fallback/state page, with no endpoint of its own. | Root unknown routes map to this screen; downstream resource 404s are represented through their caller's safe error state where applicable. | DD defines not-found navigation. Root route coverage verifies unknown URLs lose the requested private path/query; no API work is expected for the fallback page. |
| SH-07 | `/operations/:operationId`; `SH07OperationService` chooses Student/Mentor/Admin authenticated API transport and requests #18 only for a valid UUID. Invalid IDs are rejected locally. | #18 validates UUID and Student/Mentor/Admin role, scopes lookup to the submitting owner, and projects only `NOTIFICATION_DISPATCH` with `QUEUED`, `FAILED`, or `SENT`. | DD approves notification-only status tracking and identifies ME-14/#63 plus AD-20/#93 producer handoffs. Generic payment/export operation tracking is not implemented; deployed API/DB behavior remains unverified. |

Shared API verification boundary: focused Study Server tests for #01–#06/#18 previously passed 95 assertions; full Study Web suite currently passes 67 files/264 tests. Both are source/mock-backed local evidence, not live API, database, browser-session, or deployment acceptance.

## SOURCE_TRACE

- ST-03 page owns loading, API response guards, profile state, editable field state, avatar validation/upload state, save validation, update sequencing, refresh-after-save, and navigation. `ST03ProfileService` is the page's only screen-specific data access collaborator and calls the registered Student API client. Presentational children may emit UI events but must not call the API or Router.
- The current focused spec covers editable name/phone with read-only email and disabled bio, supported-field save plus refresh, and empty-name rejection. It does not exercise avatar selection/upload or error/retry paths; those remain existing coverage gaps.
- The page's custom StudentLayout mode leaves this page responsible for the full screen frame. Header/footer, avatar panel, and details form can be isolated as UI children without changing route, API, or state ownership.
- Shared screen-to-endpoint mappings are recorded above; API #02's stub provider is the material release blocker, while API #03's current source contract and the SH-04 catalog note have been reconciled to source.

## IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN

- Keep the ST-03 page as the owner of routing, loading, profile state, API orchestration, validation and save/upload decisions.
- Move the profile frame, avatar panel, and profile form into screen-local presentation components in the existing `ST03/ui/` folder; inputs carry view state and outputs carry user events back to the page. Do not add API or Router dependencies to child UI.
- Keep the StudentLayout `custom` mode and current ST-03 shell structure. Remove only unused diagnostic/avatar-lock styles and split component CSS so every Angular component style remains below the 8 kB production error budget.
- Preserve current screen interactions and API request bodies. Do not reconcile the DD's static Gate G-03 screenshot with the current wired page in this style/build target.
- Reconcile the stale API registry, route, and AD-01 test harness expectations with current source, then rerun the affected specs and full client suite.
- Continue each screen group crosswalk only after the current-source test baseline is restored.
- Complete the Shared screen-to-endpoint crosswalk; correct the stale API #01–#03 catalog text and SH-04 source update using current server source, shared screen services, and DD integration/status sections.
- Correct the Study Web endpoint catalog's current-source notes for Guest APIs #07 and #10 while retaining the distinct design contract: category is rejected by current #07 source, and current #10 source does not apply its accepted rating parameter.
- Refresh the project and web scope router summaries that still claim Guest/Mentor routes are absent, SH-04 is static, and SH-07 does not call #18; validate the `.agents` context registry after those edits.
- Remove completed leaves from the active target tracker and delete the Study Web manifest's missing `features/auth/.gitkeep` source reference; rerun the context validator and distinguish unrelated repository-wide failures.

- Current-source DD reconciliation: updated AD-01 and AD-24 overview/API-integration evidence to show local API #101 source and AD-01 caller, while keeping the AD-24 caller absent, API #102 unwired, and all Draft metric/permission/mapping decisions open.
- AD-16 required semantics: the create-mode course selector now exposes native `required` semantics to match its visible DD required marker; its existing selected-course guard and #88 request flow are unchanged. Corrected the AD-16 business-rule current-source summary to reflect the existing #73/#88 wiring and retained all nested-schema/edit gates.
- Student route coverage: added a table-driven assertion for all 26 current Student route entries, checking unique path registration, the page component, API ID metadata, and shell mode.
- Shared/Guest route coverage: added explicit source route-table assertions for Shared SH-01–SH-07 and the route-backed Guest screens GS-01–GS-03/GS-07; the existing GS-03 host owns the query-tab screens GS-04–GS-06.
- Student style ownership: removed ST-01–ST-10 page styles from Angular's global initial-style list; attached component-scoped styles to the four pages that had no local stylesheet, and converted page host selectors to `:host`. Existing per-page selector rules and shared `student-api-page.css` ownership are preserved.
- Admin style duplication: removed AD-18's page stylesheet from the global style list because `AD18Page` already owns it through `styleUrl`; production initial CSS is reduced without changing its page CSS rules.
- Admin style audit: 20 Admin page styles were globally registered; AD-18 already had a local page `styleUrl` and its duplicate global entry was removed. AD-17 (5.67 kB raw) and AD-23 (7.65 kB raw) now own their host-prefixed styles in their lazy pages using `ViewEncapsulation.None`, leaving 17 page styles global. Their emitted styles are 5.05 kB and 6.83 kB, under the 8 kB error budget. Several remaining CSS files are 9.4–15.1 kB raw and need a smaller component/view boundary before moving under that budget.
- Admin lazy CSS: AD-17 and AD-23 stylesheets now travel with their lazy page chunks instead of the initial global stylesheet; the initial bundle is 474.39 kB.
- Admin pagination semantics: the shared list paginator is now a named `<nav>` landmark; its summary and previous/next controls are unchanged. A focused `AdminScreenSurface` spec verifies the landmark name and both buttons.
- Feature pagination semantics: AD-06/10/15/16/19/20/22 and ME-13 now render their labeled pagers as native `nav` landmarks; classes, controls, request/page state, and CSS layout selectors are unchanged. A bounded search of all Study feature HTML found no remaining named pagination containers on generic `div` elements.
- Study template accessible-name semantics: corrected 13 generic elements across AD-02/03/06/16, ME-02/05, SH-02/03, and ST-03/04/05/06. The account-link wrappers now use named `nav`, AD-03 uses `dl` for its definition rows, the labeled image/status/group nodes expose matching roles, and AD-06 no longer repeats its visible label as `aria-label`. No API or business behavior changed.
- Study form-control name audit: parsed all feature templates and verified accessible names for native buttons and labels for input/textarea/select controls. A first regex result on ST-14 was a false positive caused by its valid multiline `</label >`; the parser-aware pass found zero unlabeled controls, so no source change was needed.
- ST-15 implementation gate: source/DD review confirms #31 submit is wired, while the required #30 flush barrier/direct-entry handoff remains unresolved. Added regressions only for source-backed confirmation, current accepted response, duplicate activation, and no blind replay after network ambiguity/409; the tests do not claim the flush barrier or recovery copy is complete. Keep the direct-entry handoff as a release blocker until its queue contract is approved.
- Study image alternatives audit: all 16 feature-template images have explicit static or bound `alt`; 14 empty alternatives belong to decorative avatars/thumbnails with adjacent course/person identity, and two bind the course title. No image-label source fix was needed.
- Study sign-in autocomplete: changed the SH-02 email identifier hint from `email` to `username`, preserving `type="email"` and all validation/submission behavior; expanded the page spec to assert `username` and `current-password`. Registration and account-creation fields already use `email`/`new-password`; a parser-aware source scan verified all 7 active credential inputs.
- Study personal-field autocomplete audit: verified eight enabled name/email/phone fields use purpose-matched tokens. AD-04 identity fields are disabled, and AD-06 course title is non-personal; no additional source changes were warranted.
- Study keyboard-focus audit: inspected all six CSS occurrences of `outline: 0`/`outline: none`; every input either has a visible `:focus-visible` rule or its enclosing search control renders a visible `:focus-within` ring. No CSS source change was needed.
- ST-18 ambiguous-result feedback: the #34 upload-uncertain and #35 submission-uncertain paths now retain their distinct existing messages, render once next to the submit action, and expose the dynamic message as `role=alert`; the duplicate page-level alert is hidden only while the current assignment is locked for an unknown outcome. No API payload, retry, or persistence policy changed.
- ST-18 upload-success feedback: the existing #34 success is now announced while #35 is pending, with state-specific copy that does not ask for a duplicate submission or announce final success before #35 confirms. The status disappears after confirmed submission; an unknown #35 result retains upload-only status plus the single uncertainty alert.
- ST-18 progress-stage feedback: the submit button now distinguishes an active #34 upload from an active #35 submission, including text-only submissions; the existing disabled guard and request order remain unchanged.
- ST-17 unresolved-policy gate: removed direct due-date/score formatting, the generic course identity claim and the usable ST-18 CTA because the DD leaves those mappings/eligibility unresolved. The page retains only #33 title/description and has a routed regression for the restriction. Updated the screen DD current-source notes; live API and authorization remain unverified.
- ST-17 files changed: `apps/study-client/src/app/features/student/ST17/pages/st17-page.html`, `apps/study-client/src/app/features/student/ST17/pages/st17-page.ts`, `apps/study-client/src/app/features/student/ST17/pages/st17-page.spec.ts`, and the ST-17 `00-overview.md`/`04-api-integration.md` current-source notes.
- ST-19 unresolved-field gate: removed local timestamp formatting and score/feedback/file presentation while their policy/asset contracts remain open; retained only server status and optional submitted text. Updated ST-19 current-source notes and Study Web context; added a routed page regression. Local runtime does not prove deployed API or release policy.
- ST-19 API mapping: added a service-level regression proving route submission ID `54` becomes API #36's `submission_id` path parameter, with no invented query/body fields.
- ST-24 current-source reconciliation: updated the DD overview/events to distinguish the prototype's unavailable state from the actual Angular form and current #20/#40/#39 callers, while retaining the catalog string-vs-int64 mismatch and the missing screenshot CTA as unresolved gates.
- ST-24 API mapping tests: added focused assertions for #20 ACTIVE course paging, #40 current-source body fields, and #39 course-scoped page-one refresh. These tests do not claim deployed API, DB or product approval.
- ST-20/ST-22 API #38 mapping: added service specs asserting each view calls #38 with no user ID or other request fields; actor identity remains session-derived in Study Server source.
- ST-26 API mapping: added a shared notification service spec through the ST-26 wrapper. It verifies #43 list query fields, #44 notification path mapping, and #45's current request with no extra fields; no read-state/deep-link/DB policy was inferred.
- ST-23 source/API mapping: added service regressions for #39 page and optional course filters, and reconciled the stale current-source status in the screen API-integration DD against the registered route, Student validator, handler, and current query. The catalog's `sort` field remains a discrepancy because the current handler ignores it.
- ST-23 page consistency: added a routed regression for a valid #39 response whose separate COUNT/LIST reads disagree; the UI retains returned discussion rows, announces the mismatch with a retry action, and hides untrustworthy pagination.
- API #39 server coverage: added route-level tests for token-derived Student identity, course/page SQL mapping, default page behavior, Page response shape, and denial of missing/non-Student credentials. Query functions are mocked; no live DB or deployed API is inferred.
- API #26 test alignment: the source already required ACTIVE course enrollment, but three older success fixtures did not stub the enrollment query. Updated those fixtures and added an explicit missing-enrollment 403 test without changing the production handler.
- ST-21 API mapping/current source: added a focused client regression for `course_id` path mapping and reconciled the stale DD `NOT_FOUND` snapshot with the registered #37 route, Student guard, enrollment query, and `CourseProgress` response. The catalog response mismatch stays open.
- ST-21 page behavior: added routed regressions for the valid API-backed aggregate (including accessible progress value and unavailable lesson details) and an invalid course ID that must not call #37.
- API #37 server coverage: added route-level tests for token-derived Student identity, ACTIVE-enrollment query mapping, aggregate count/percentage projection, and missing/non-Student authentication responses. DB reads are mocked; no live database or deployment is verified.
- ST-01 error/retry coverage: the page already renders separate named widget error groups with nested `role=alert` messages and retry buttons. Added regression coverage for all three error emissions and each isolated retry; no page/API behavior needed changing.
- ST-15 regression coverage: added a routed page spec for explicit confirmation, accepted-response navigation, pending-submit duplicate suppression, and no blind retry/navigation after status 0 or 409; added a service spec for API #31 and `attempt_id` mapping. The page tests intentionally leave the unresolved #30 flush barrier and recovery-copy decisions open.
- ME-12 regression and source reconciliation: added six routed page tests and two #59/#60 service mapping tests; corrected the DD current-source claims and test evidence, Study Web index, and Mentor crosswalk. Current reply/attachment and resolve gates remain explicit; these local tests do not establish live API/DB/runtime readiness.
- AD-03 page coverage: added four routed client tests for the authenticated #69 detail read, signed-int64 route rejection, HTTP failure, wrong business-code rejection, and the disabled AD-04/AD-05 controls. Updated the AD-03 overview/test-case evidence, Study Web index and screen-spec coverage audit. No production page code changed; the local tests do not close the permission, PII, audit/schema or runtime gates.
- AD-13 page coverage: added five routed client tests for API #83 request/auth, supported fields, plain-text content, invalid IDs/projections, failure/business-code handling, unavailable resources/metrics and the disabled AD-14 action. Updated current AD-13 test evidence; dependency, permission, schema, media and runtime blockers remain open.
- ME-02 page coverage: added five routed HTTP-backed client tests for authenticated API #46, supported Course fields, loading/status filtering, out-of-range recovery, invalid projections, and retry. Updated the ME-02 overview/test-cases, Study Web index, current Mentor API crosswalk, and page-spec audit (12 routed gaps remain). No production page source changed; assignment, mockup-only fields/actions, live API/DB and runtime gates remain open.
- ME-03 workspace coverage and response handling: added five routed HTTP-backed tests for #46/#49/#65, supported projections, invalid route IDs, cross-panel access denial, and server/validation error messages. Fixed the page to show generic screen validation errors for malformed successful envelopes while preserving messages from `success:false` responses. Refreshed ME-03 overview/events/data-flow/API/test evidence, Study Web index and page-spec audit (11 route-backed gaps remain). DD #65/permission decisions and live API/DB/runtime evidence remain open.
- ME-04 routed coverage: created tests for authenticated #46 course loading, #34 multipart upload, #47 create request/navigation, invalid route IDs, and fail-closed ambiguous upload/create outcomes. Focused verification is pending; no production page source changed.
- Context pages changed: `.agents/project/architecture.md`, `.agents/web-work/INDEX.md`, `.agents/context-map.md`, `.agents/context-manifest.json`.
- Assumptions: “Study UI” refers to `apps/study-client` until registered context or source shows another scope.
- Target progress updates and completion evidence: see `TARGET_UPDATES`.

## HANDOFF

- Route verification: current route tables and focused assertions cover all 83 Study screens across Shared, Guest, Student, Mentor, and Admin; GS-04–GS-06 are hosted as GS-03 query tabs rather than independent router entries. The latest Study Web suite passes 104 spec files/375 tests, including ST-11, ST-18, ME-02, ME-03, ME-12, AD-03, and AD-13 page/service regressions and the existing ST-12 coverage. Eleven routed pages still lack sibling page specs. This is local source/mock coverage, not authenticated browser acceptance.
- Remaining blockers: The route/API caller crosswalk covers all 83 Study screens, but several DDs block or defer behavior, including user/course/content/lesson/quiz/notification/moderation writes and many loaded-state designs. Student achievement/notification queries also conflict with the checked-in pilot DDL; the DB Admin schema context is missing. Current Study API, live database, browser session, object storage, delivery provider, export storage, and deployment runtime are unverified. The production build is below its initial-bundle budget; 18 component-style warnings remain, including `mentor-pages.css` at 4.02 kB, while ST-26's warning is cleared.
- Next owner/action: provide product/API decisions for blocked DD fields, metric definitions, authorization, mutation/lifecycle rules, response shapes and export delivery; then implement the approved UI/API slices and verify against an available runtime. Do not enable or derive the blocked behaviors from mockup copy alone.
